# The Book of Maccabees II

ספר מקבים ב

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II)

תקופת ההלניזם, רדיפת היהודים בימי שלטון אנטיוכוס וקרבות יהודה המקבי.

Accounts of hellenization, persecution of Jews under Antiochus, and battles of Judah Maccabee.

Sections: Chapter-Verse

Location: Second Temple/Apocrypha

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): כתובים אחרונים - יצחק זעקיל פרענקיל (Segments: 660, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): Sefaria Community Translation (Segments: 110)


## Chapter 1

###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:1)


היהודים אשר בירושלים ובארץ יהודה לאחיהם היהודים במצרים שלום וישע רב!


...The Jews in Jerusalem and in the land of Judah to their Jewish brethren in Egypt, peace and salvation!


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:2)


ה' אלוהים יברך אתכם, ויזכור לכם את בריתו את אברהם ואת יצחק ואת יעקב.


God bless you, and remember you his covenant with Abraham and Isaac and Jacob.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:3)


ויתן לכם לב שלם ליראה אותו ולעבדו בכל לבבכם ובכל נפשכם.


And give you a whole heart to fear him and to serve him with all your heart and all your soul.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:4)


יפתח ה' לבבכם להגות בתורתו ולשמור מצוותיו, ויתן לכם ברכה ושלום.


God will open your hearts to reflect on His Torah and keep His commandments, and will give you a blessing and peace.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:5)


לו ישמע ה' את תפילתכם וירחם עליכם, ומכל צרה יפלט נפשכם.


If only The Lord will listen to your prayers and be merciful to you, and from hardship He will save your lives.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:6)


זאת תפילתנו בעדכם כל הימים.


This is our prayer to You for all time.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:7)


בשנת תשע ושישים ומאה, עת מלך מלך דימטריוס, כתבנו לכם את כל הצרות אשר עברו על נפשנו, בבגוד ישוע ועדתו בארץ הקדושה ויפשעו בממלכות יהודה.


In the year 169, the time of the reign of King Demetrius, we wrote to you concerning all the hardship that came upon us, with the betrayal of Yeshua and his cohorts in the the holy land, they sinned in the kingdom of Judah.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:8)


ויציתו את שער החצר באש וישפכו דם נקי בארץ.


They set ablaze the gate to the courtyard. They spilled innocent blood in the land.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:9)


ונתפלל את ה' אלוהינו ויקשב לקולנו, ונקרב לו קורבן וסולת למנחה, נעלה נרות וניתן את לחם הפנים על שולחן ה'.


We prayed to The Lord our God and he headed our voice. We offered sacrifices and fine flour for the Mincha offering. We lit the lamps, and placed the show-bread on the table of The Lord.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:10)


ושאלנו מכם לחוג עמנו את חנוכת המזבח בירח כסלו.


We requested of you to celebrate the dedication of the altar with us during the month of Kislev.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:11)


ויהי בשנת מאה שמונים ושמונה, וישובו ויכתבו להם את הדברים האלה.


It was in the year 188, They returned and they wrote for them these words.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:12)


אנשי יהודה וירושלים והזקנים, לאריסטובלוס אומֵן המלך תלמי ממטה הכוהנים המשוחים, וליהודים אשר במצרים, שלום וישע.


[From the] People of Judah and Jerusalem and the elders, to Aristobulos, ?Secretary?(Meaning of word uncertain) of Ptolemy the king, from the tribe of the anointed priests, and to the Jews of Egypt, Peace and Salvation.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:13)


הננו מברכים את ה' אלוהינו אשר הציל את נפשנו מצרה גדולה להתייצב לפני מלך אדיר ונורא.


We are blessing The Lord our God who saved our lives from great distress [so that we would be able] to stand before the mighty and awesome king.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:14)


כי הוריש ה' את אויבינו מעירנו הקדושה, ויפיצם בגבולי ארץ פרס.


For The Lord has removed our enemies from our holy city, He scattered them into the borders of Persia.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:15)


והמלך עם חילו הגדול והעצום נהרגו בבית נאניה בעקבות כוהניה.


And the king with his great and mighty army were killed at the house of Naniah following her priests.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:16)


כי בבואו אנטיוכוס עם להקת מרעיו אל בית נאניה, ויתלוצץ לקחת אותה לו לאשה, למען החזיק באוצרותיה כמוהר.


For when Antiochus brought ?an army to gather his vassals?(Meaning of words uncertain) to the house of Naniah, he joked to take her as a wife in order to take her treasure houses as a dowry.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:17)


ויהי כאשר הביאו כוהני נאניה את האוצר, והמלך ואנשיו הלכו אל הבית פנימה לראותו, ויסגרו הכוהנים את דלתי הבית מאחריהם.


It was as the priests of Naniah brought him to the treasure house, the king and his men went into the inner chamber to see it, the priests closed the doors of the chamber behind them.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:18)


ויפתחו דלת נסתרה, ויסקלו אותו וכל אשר עמו באבנים וימותו.


They opened a hidden door, and they stoned him and everyone with him with stones and they died.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:19)


ויכרתו את ראשם ויגזרו אתם לגזרים, וישליכום החוצה.


They cut off their heads, they cut them up into pieces, and they threw them outside.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:20)


הודות והלל לה' כי הכרית את הרשעים מן הארץ ואינם.


Thanks and praise to The Lord, for he has cut off the wicked from the land. They are no more.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:21)


ועתה כי יש את נפשנו לחוג את יום חנוכת המזבח בעשרים וחמש לחודש כסלו. לא חדלנו מהודיע אתכם לחוג אותו עמנו.


And now we desire to celebrate the day of the dedication of the altar on the 25th of the month of Kislev. We did not refrain from notifying you to celebrate it with us.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:22)


וחגותם אותו כימי חג הסוכות, וכיום אשר מצא בו נחמיה את אש הקודש בשובו לבנות את המקדש ואת המזבח, ויקרב עליו עולות וזבחים לאלוהים.


You will celebrate it like the days of the festival of Sukkot, and like the day on which Nehemiah found the holy fire upon his return to build the Temple and the altar. He offered on it burnt offerings and sacrifices to God.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:23)


כי כאשר הוגלו אבותינו ארצה פרס, לקחו הכוהנים הקדושים את האש בסתר מעל המזבח, ויטמנו אותה בשוחה עמוקה ויבשה לשומרה, ולא ידע איש את מקומה.


For when our forefathers were exiled to Persia, the holy priests secretly took the fire from upon the altar, and they hid it in a deep and dry pit to preserve it. No man knew its location.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:24)


ויהי מקץ ימים רבים, בנטות ה' את חסדו עלינו, והמלך שלח את נחמיה ירושלימה.


It was at the end of many days, when the Lord pitched his kindness upon us, and the king sent Nehemiah to Jerusalem.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:25)


ויקח מבני הכוהנים אשר טמנו את האש, וישלחם לבקְשה.


He took descendants of the priests who hid the fire, and he sent them to search for it.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:26)


אולם כאשר הוגד לנו לא מצאו את האש, כי אם מים קפואים מצאו תחתיה, ויצו נחמיה לשאוב את המים ולהביאם.


But when it was told to us that they didn't find the fire, rather frozen water beneath it, Nehemiah commanded [them] to draw the water and to bring it.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:27)


ויהי בהקריבם את קורבן ה', ויצו אותם לזרוק מן המים על העצים ועל הקורבן אשר על המזבח, ויעשו כן.


It was when they were offering the sacrifice of the Lord, he commanded them to throw the water on the wood and on the sacrifice that was on the altar. They did so.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:28)


אחרי כלותם, והשמש יצאה על הארץ, והעבים נפוצו, והנה אש אלוקים מתלקחת בקורבן, וישתומם כל העם מסביב.


After they finished [pouring the water], the sun came out over the land, and the clouds scattered. Behold a fire of God engulfed the sacrifice! The entire crowd around was awe struck.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:29)


ויפלו הכוהנים וכל העם וישתחוו, עד כי אוּכל הקורבן.


The priests and the people fell and they prostrated, until the sacrifice was consumed.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:30)


ויתפלל יונתן אל ה', ונחמיה וכל העם ענו אחריו ויאמרו.


Jonathan prayed to the Lord, and Nehemiah and the people responded after him; they said:


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:31)


ה' אלוקים יוצר ובורא הכל, עזוז ונורא צדיק ורחום, אתה לבדך מלכינו ומושיענו.


The Lord our God, molder and creator of all, Powerful and Awesome, Righteous and Merciful, You alone are our King and our Savior.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:32)


ה' הצדיק הפותח ידו הרחבה לכל חי, אלוקי האלוקים ואדון העולם, הפודה את עמו ישראל מכל צרותיו, אתה בחרת באבותינו וקידשת אותם לעבודתך.


The Righteous Lord who opens his wide hand for all life, The God over all Gods and Master of the world, The One who redeems Israel from all his troubles, You chose our forefathers and You sanctified them for Your service.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:33)


רצה נא את הקורבן הזה בעד עמך ישראל, ושמר את נחלתך וברכה.


Desire now this sacrifice on behalf of Your people Israel, and guard Your portion and bless it.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:34)


קבץ את פזורי עמך ופדה אותם מיד הגויים, פקח עינך וראה את חרפתם, כי תועבת כל אדם המה, למען ידעו הגויים כי אתה ה' אלוהינו.


Gather the scattered of Your nation and redeem them from the hand of the nations. Open Your eyes and see their shame, for they are the disgust of all mankind. [Do this] In order that the nations will know that You, The Lord, are our God.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:35)

Pour Your wrath upon the oppressors of Your nation, that with stubbornness of their hearts crushed us to the ground.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:36)

Bring us and plant us in the mountain of Your portion, like Moses Your servant said.

When they finished supplicating, the priests sang praise and thanks to The Lord.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:38)


ויהי כי אוכל הקורבן, ויצו נחמיה לצקת את המים הנותרים על הרצפה, ותלהט להבה ותבלע אותה האש מעל המזבח.


It was when the sacrifice was consumed, Nehemiah commanded [them] to spill the remaining water on the floor, a flame burned and the fire from on the altar swallowed it.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 1:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:39)

It was told to the king of Persia that water was found in the place that the exiled priests hid the fire, and that fire burned a sacrifice that Nehemiah offered. He searched and investigated to see if it was true.

He found that it was true and correct. He sanctified the place and fenced it off, and he gave expensive gifts to all who found favor in his eyes.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/1:41)

Nehemiah called that place Hefter, saying that it is holy. Others called it Nifter.



## Chapter 2

###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:1)


וגם נמצא כתוב בספרים, כי ירמיהו הנביא ציוה לגולים לקחת את האש ולטמנה כאשר אמרנו.


It was also written in the books, that Jeremiah the prophet commanded the exiles to take the fire and hide it like we said.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:2)


גם נתן להם את ספר תורת ה' לבל ישכחוה, ולבבם לא יפתה לסור מן הדרך, בראותם את פסילי זהב וכסף בגאון תפארתם.


[Jeremiah] also gave them the book of the Torah of The Lord so they should not forget it, and they should not be seduced in their hearts to turn from the path, when they see graven images of gold and silver with the pride of their glory.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:3)


וישנן להם דברים רבים ונכוחים, לבל תמוש תורת ה' מלבבם.


[Jeremiah] taught them many proven things, so that the Torah of The Lord would not leave their hearts.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:4)


וגם זאת נמצא כתוב, כי הגיד להם על פי ה', לשאת אתם את אוהל מועד ואת הארון.


This was also written, that [Jeremiah] told them according to the mouth of The Lord, to take with them The Tent of Meeting and The Ark.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:5)


ויהי בבואם ההרה אשר עלה עליו משה לראות את הארץ, וימצא ירמיהו שם מערה ויסתר בה את האוהל ואת הארון ואת מזבח הקטורת, ויסתום את פיה.


It was when they came to the mountain that Moses ascended to see the land, Jeremiah found a cave there and he hid The Tent of Meeting, The Ark, and The Alter of Incense in it, and he closed [the cave's] opening.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:6)


ומקצת האנשים אשר הלכו אתו בקשו לעשות להם אות, וילאו למצוא את המערה.


Some of the people that went with [Jeremiah] tried to make a sign [at the cave] for themselves, yet they became tired from looking for the cave.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:7)


וישמע ירמיהו ויגער בם ויאמר, אל ידע איש את המקום, עד אשר יקבץ ה' את עמו ונתן להם רחמים.


Jeremiah heard [about it] and he rebuked them and said. "No man will know the location [of the cave], until The Lord will gather his nation and grant them mercy.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:8)


אז יגלה ה' את המקום, וכבוד ה' יופיע בענן, כאשר היה בימי משה ובימי שלמה בהתחננו אל ה' לקדשו.


Then The Lord will reveal the location [of the cave], and the glory of The Lord will shine in a cloud, as it was in the days of Moses and Solomon when they begged The Lord to sanctify ?himself? (unclear pronoun)."


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:9)


ויספר להם ירמיהו את חכמת שלמה ואת הקורבנות אשר הקריב עת חנוכת המזבח, ככלותו להקים את מקדש ה'.


Jeremiah told them about the wisdom of Solomon and about the sacrifices that [Solomon] offered [at] the time of the dedication of the alter, when he finished erecting the temple of The Lord.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:10)


וכאשר התפלל משה, ותצא אש מלפני ה' ותאכל על המזבח את העולה, כן נענה גם שלמה בתפילתו.


When Moses prayed, A fire left from the presence The Lord and consumed the burnt offering that was on the alter. In this manner Solomon's prayer was also answered.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:11)


כי אש ירדה מן השמיים ותאכל את העולה ואת הזבחים.


For a fire descended from the heavens and consumed the burnt offering and the sacrifices.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:12)


וכאשר דרש דרש משה את שעיר החטאת והנה שורף ולא אכל ממנו איש, כן עשה שלמה בהקריבו את קורבנותיו לה' שמונת ימים.


And when Moses searched [?in the ashes for?] the ram of the sin offering, Behold! it was charred and no man ate of it. So did Solomon when he offered his sacrifices to The Lord [all] eight days.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:13)


כל הדברים האלה נמצאו בדברי הימים הנכתבים בימי נחמיה.


All these events can be found in The Chronicles that were written in the days of Nehemiah.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:14)


וכאשר דרש נחמיה אל ספרי דוד והמלכים והנביאים, ואת אשר כתבו בדבר הקורבנות ויאספם לאסיפה.


When Nehemiah searched for the books of David, the kings, the prophets, and [anything] that had information about the sacrifices, he collected them into a collection.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:15)


כן עשה גם יהודה, כי אסוף אסף את הספרים אשר נפוצו והתפזרו בסערת המלחמה בארץ.


So did Judah, when he collected the books that were scattered and dispersed in the storm of the war throughout the land.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:16)


והם למשמרת בידינו, ואם נכספתם להם, שלחו נא אלינו ושלחנום לכם.


They are being guarded in our hands, and if you are desiring of them, send to us and they will be sent to you.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:17)


ועתה כתבנו לכם את כל הדברים האלה, למען תחוגו עמנו את החג הזה כאשר יאות לכם.


Now, we wrote for you all of these things, so that you will celebrate this festival with us according to what is fit for you.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:18)


והננו מייחלים לה' אלוהינו אשר פדה את עמו, והשיב לנו את נחלתנו ואת הממלכה ואת עבודת הכהונה כאשר הבטיחנו בתורתו הקדושה.


We are hoping of The Lord our God that he will redeem his nation, and return to us our portion, the kingdom, and the service of the priests that was promised in His holy Torah.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:19)


כן ירחם עלינו ויקבץ נידחנו מתחת כל השמיים, להביא אותם אל ארץ קודשינו, כאשר החל לחלץ אותנו ממצוקותינו ויטהר את בית מקדשו.


So he should be merciful upon us and gather our dispersed from under the heavens, to bring them to our holy land, just like he has begun to remove us from our distant places, and he purified his temple.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:20)


ואת מעשי יהודה המכבי ואחיו ואת טהרת המקדש מטומאתו, ואת חנוכת הבית ואת מלחמות אנטיוכוס המפואר ובנו אאופטר.


And the deeds of Judah Maccabeus, his brothers, the purification of the temple from its impurity, the dedication ceremony of the temple, and the wars against Antiochus the great and his son Aopater.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:21)


ואותות השמיים אשר ראו האצילים והמקנאים לעדת ישראל.


And the heavenly signs that the strong and zealous saw for the congregation of Israel.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:22)


איכה גדודי מצער הניסו חיל גדול מן הגויים, וירדפו אחריהם ויבזו את ארצם.


How is it that my tiny battalion chased the massive legions of the nations? They pursued them and they disgraced their land.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:23)


ואת פדות קריתנו הקדושה ואת בנין הבית המפואר על פני כל הארץ.


And the redemption of our holy city, and the construction of the house whose splendor covers the entire world.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:24)


וכאשר הוקמו בחסד ה' עלינו כל חוקותיו ומשפטיו אשר כמעט הופרו בארץ.


And when The Lord established upon us with kindness his rules and laws that had almost been dissolved throughout the land.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:25)


את כל הדברים האלה אשר כתבם ישוע הקריני על ספרים חמישה, ניסיתי לכלכל בדברים מעטים ולהעלותם על ספר אחד.


All these words that Jesus the Cyrenean wrote on five scrolls, I tried to condense his words and deliver them on one scroll.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:26)


כי בראותי את מספר המעשים כי בלול, וכבדו הדברים על הקורא כי רבו.


For when I saw that the accounts of events were mixed up, and that its many words were burdensome to the reader.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:27)


חשבתי לספרם כמשפטם, לבעבור ירוץ הקורא בהם, ויקל עליו להבינם ולשומרם בלבו.


I decided to retell [the events] in their proper order, in order that the reader would run through them, and that it would be easy [for the reader] to understand them and remember them.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:28)


ידעתי גם ידעתי, כי בצאתי לקצר רבה וכבדה המלאכה עלי, וזעת אפים ולילות עמל הכינותי לי.


I certainly knew that in my endeavor to abridge [the book of Jesus the Cyrenean] a great and heavy task befell me; the sweat of my brow and nights of hard work were prepared for me.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:29)


אולם כי יעשה איש משתה, גם עליו תיכבד העבודה להשלים חפצי כל קרואיו.


Yet when a man will make a feast, the responsibilities of catering to the desires of his guests also fall upon him.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:30)


לכן לא יראתי להעמיס את העבודה על שכמי, להועיל לכל אשר יחפוץ בה.


Therefore I was not afraid to burden my shoulders with the work, [because it would] benefit all who would desire it.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:31)


ועזבתי את המעשים האחדים במתכונתם לסופר הראשון, ורק לקצר הדברים עמלתי.


I left some of the stories in the format of the original author, my work was only to abridge his words.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:32)


וכאשר יעשה החרש, בבנותו בית ידאג וישכיל על יסודי הבית ותכונותיו, ויעזוב הטיבו ויפותו לאיש אחר, כן עשיתי אף אני.


This is just like the work of a carpenter, when he builds a house he worries and thinks about the foundation and structure of the house, and he leaves the decoration and beautification[of the house] to another person. This is what I did.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:33)


כי דרך כל סופרי הקורות, לדרוש ולחקור לכל עניין ומעשה, לכלכל הדברים כמשפט ולהשתרע כחפצם.


For the way of all historians is to search and investigate every incident and event, to collect everything in order and to organize [the events] according to his desire.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:34)


אך למקצר ניתן חוק לערוך את עצם הקורות, ולהציגם לפני הקורא בדרך קצרה.


But for one who abridges there is a given rule to lay out the main part of the events, and to display them before the reader in a brief manner.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 2:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/2:35)


ואחרי הקדמתי זאת אפנה את לבי לדברי הימים, למען לא יגדל מפתח הספר מתוכן דבריו.


And after I began this, I turned my attention to the chronicles, ?in order that the key of the book would not grow from within his words? (translation uncertain)



## Chapter 3

ויהי כי ירא מפניו, וילך אל אפלוניוס בן תרשי נציב ארם וצור, ויאמר לו.


###### The Book of Maccabees II 3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/3:6)

###### The Book of Maccabees II 3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/The%20Book%20of%20Maccabees%20II/r/3:7)
